ideas = {
	companies_slot = {
		#GENERIC COMPANIES
		Generic_Automobilecompany_idea = {
			on_add = {
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 0.3 }
				change_domestic_influence_percentage = yes
				GENERIC_count_companies_script = yes
				log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: add idea Generic_Automobilecompany_idea"
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
				change_oligarchs_opinion = yes
				hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_count_companies = 1 }
					modify_count_companies_support = yes 
				}
			}
			allowed = { always = yes }
			allowed_civil_war = { always = yes }
			picture = generic_company_auto_idea

			on_remove = {
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -0.3 }
				change_domestic_influence_percentage = yes
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -5 }
				change_oligarchs_opinion = yes
				hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_count_companies = -1 }
					modify_count_companies_support = yes 
				}
			}

			visible = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = SOV
				}
			}

			modifier = { 
				production_speed_industrial_complex_factor = 0.04 
			}
			equipment_bonus = {
				util_vehicle_equipment = {
					instant = yes
					reliability = 0.02
					build_cost_ic = -0.03
				}
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				factor = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 200
					NOT = {
						OR = {
							original_tag = SOV
							original_tag = USA
							original_tag = GER
							original_tag = FRA
							original_tag = ENG
							original_tag = AZE
							original_tag = ARM
							original_tag = BLR
							original_tag = CHI
							original_tag = TAI
							original_tag = POL
							original_tag = SLO
							original_tag = HUN
							original_tag = POR
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
